                  exploit). New utils/orderInventory.priceOrderItemsFromDb() recomputes each item price from the DB
                  (variant.price else product.price) + trusted subtotal; combos KEEP their allocated price (tagged with
                  combo_id from checkout). orderRoutes now uses server prices/subtotal/total; coupon discount already
                  server-side. For ONLINE, it fetches the Razorpay order and rejects if amount != server total.
                  Verified: a tampered ₹1 order saved as the real ₹399.
                  (C) #2 CREATE-PENDING-ORDER-BEFORE-PAY + reserve + auto-cleanup. New flow: createRazorpayOrder →
                  create our order as PENDING (stock reserved, amount validated) BEFORE the popup → pay → verify marks
                  paid → navigate. razorpayService split into createRazorpayOrder() + public openCheckout(). On
                  abandon/fail the frontend calls POST /api/orders/:id/cancel (new) → restores stock + reverses coupon +
                  marks cancelled (ownership-checked). SAFETY NET utils/pendingOrders.cleanupStalePendingOrders()
                  scheduled in server.js (~1min after boot, then every 15min): sweeps online orders 'pending' >45min —
                  double-checks Razorpay and RECONCILES to paid if actually paid, else releases. Fixed a real bug:
                  restoreInventoryForOrderItems only restored PRODUCT stock — now restores VARIANT + JSONB sizes too.
                  Dup-guard added by razorpay_order_id. Verified cancel restores variant+product stock.
                  (D) RAZORPAY WEBHOOK (POST /api/payment/webhook) — signature-verified (RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET, raw
                  body captured in server.js), idempotent, marks matching order paid / logs orphan payments. Tested.

====================================================
SECTION 1 : WHAT THIS PROJECT IS
====================================================

ORIGIN:
  This project started as "YuvicCosmetics" — a full-stack Indian
  cosmetics e-commerce site adapted from a hospital management system
  (PatientERP/Cliniva). We are NOT rebuilding from scratch.
  We are rebranding and extending the existing codebase.

GOAL:
  Transform it into CAVERO FRAGRANCES — a luxury Arabian/Oud/Attar
  perfume e-commerce store for the Indian market.

BUSINESS DETAILS:
  Brand Name  : Cavero Fragrances
  Owner       : Manthan Gadara
  Location    : Surat, Gujarat, India
  Email       : caverofragrance@gmail.com
  Phone       : 9274521140
  Instagram   : https://www.instagram.com/caverofragrance
  WhatsApp    : 919274521140

AESTHETIC:
  (UPDATED Session 36 — recolored gold→GREEN palette, 4 colors only:)
    cream      #FFF7E4  (light backgrounds / surfaces)
    mint       #8DCFB4  (light accent / highlights / hovers)
    teal       #418576  (PRIMARY accent — was the gold #C9A84C role)
    dark-green #1A322D  (dark surfaces / cards / dark text)
  Kept: white/black/transparency (text, shadows, glass) + semantic status colors
  (red errors / green success / blue info). White/black are NOT brand colors.
  ORIGINAL (pre-S36): gold #C9A84C, cream #FFF8E7, deep golden-brown #8B6914.
  Logo: assets/images/cavero-logo.svg is still a GOLD wordmark (SVG not swept) —
  reads fine on dark; re-color it if a green logo is wanted. Backup of pre-recolor
  src at .theme-backup-src/ (delete once happy).

====================================================
SECTION 2 : QUICK START
====================================================

  ONE COMMAND to start everything:
    cd "C:\Users\DELL\Desktop\keval\Cavero Fragrances"
    npm run dev

  This starts:
    [BACKEND]  Node.js/Express   → http://localhost:5000  (yellow logs)
    [FRONTEND] Angular 19        → http://localhost:4200  (cyan logs)
  Press Ctrl+C once to stop both.

  Admin Panel:
    URL   : http://localhost:4200/authentication/signin   ← EXACT URL
    Email : admin@cavero.com
    Pass  : Admin@123

  ⚠ DO NOT use http://localhost:4200/signin — that is the CUSTOMER page.
    The admin login is at /authentication/signin only.

  Verify backend is running:
    Open browser → http://localhost:5000/api/auth/categories/frontend
    Should return 7 perfume categories as JSON.

  Re-seed if database is wiped:
    node backend/auth-service/scripts/seed.js
    node backend/auth-service/scripts/seed-products-cms.js
    (see Section 9 for full re-seed steps)

  Run Browser Tests (Playwright):
    ⚠ App must be running first (npm run dev)
    One-time setup : npx playwright install   (downloads ~300MB browser binaries)
    All browsers   : npm test
    Visible windows: npm run test:headed
    Chrome only    : npm run test:chrome
    Mobile only    : npm run test:mobile
    HTML Report    : npm run test:report

    Session 31 COMPLETED (Symphony section → rotating image showcase, 2026-06-13):
      Owner finalised the Symphony direction (was "undecided" in Session 30). Rebuilt the
      "A Symphony of Fragrance" section as a continuous, auto-rotating showcase.
      All files: src/themes/cavero/components/featured-categories/featured-categories.component.{ts,html,scss}

      THE MODEL (owner's exact spec):
      • 5 "slots": a FIXED left featured card (slot 0) + a horizontal ROW of small cards (slots 1..n-1).
      • Rotating set = [Symphony intro, ...categories]. Every 4.5s the whole set advances ONE slot.
        Order example: [Symphony][Men][Women][Unisex][Oud] → [Men][Women][Unisex][Oud][Symphony] → …loop.
      • The item that lands in slot 0 becomes the featured card content (cross-fade); the previous
        featured wraps to the end of the row. Right row slides left via translate3d, then SNAPS back
        (transition removed) after the 1.3s slide while the index advances → seamless infinite loop,
        no reflow, no resize. trackBy index keeps n card nodes fixed; only the transform moves.

      IMPLEMENTATION NOTES (featured-categories.component.ts):
      • ShowcaseItem[] items = [introItem, ...categoryItems]; visibleCount = items.length-1.
      • rotation counter; recomputeTrack() builds trackItems = [items[(rot+1+j)%n] for j in 0..n-1]
        (the n-1 non-featured cards + the current featured pre-rendered off-screen-right as the incoming).
      • advance(): measureSlot() = card[1].offsetLeft - card[0].offsetLeft (exact px incl. gap);
        set featOut/featIn for the cross-fade, animating=true, trackShift=-slot; after SLIDE_MS (1300)
        rotation++, animating=false, trackShift=0, recompute. Timer runs via NgZone.runOutsideAngular.
      • KILL-SWITCH: prefersReducedMotion = matchMedia(reduce) || navigator.webdriver → no rotation,
        fully static (Session 29 convention). HTML root gets [class.cavero-motion]="!prefersReducedMotion"
        so all continuous CSS animations are gated off under reduced-motion/Playwright.

      LAYOUT / CSS:
      • Left featured = fixed card, min-height clamp(330px,33vw,420px); content swap uses absolutely-
        stacked .cavero-symphony-feat-layer (is-in / is-out) → pure opacity cross-fade, ZERO reflow.
      • Right = .cavero-symphony-track-wrap (overflow:hidden, clips the incoming card) > .track (flex,
        nowrap, gap 1rem). Card slot width: flex 0 0 calc((100% - (--vis-1)*gap)/--vis); --vis bound
        from visibleCount. Responsive: 3 cards @≤991px, 2 cards @≤575px (flex-basis overrides).
      • Small cards = full-bleed image (object-fit:cover) + bottom gradient + name/count overlaid, on a
        #1A1510 base → every tile reads as the SAME solid full-height card (matched the intro tile size;
        the earlier cream-label cards only LOOKED shorter because the label blended into the white box).
      • Intro tile in the row = dark card, centred gold "A Symphony of Fragrance" + "Shop All ›".

      ITERATION HISTORY (owner feedback, in order — important so we don't re-litigate):
      1. First attempt = big CENTER carousel (one large item) → REJECTED. Owner wanted the existing grid
         layout kept, all items rotating in place. Reverted.
      2. Built the slot-rotation showcase above. ✓
      3. "image showing in entire content area" → featured shows category image FULL-BLEED (bg) with text
         overlaid + dark scrim.
      4. "size same as category" → converted small cards to full-bleed image tiles (all uniform 420px).
      5. "enhance — animation/smoothness/motion" → added Ken Burns, staggered featured reveal, synced gold
         PROGRESS LINE (.featured::after, 4500ms loop), CTA sheen, up-next glow on first row card, hover
         lift, easeInOutQuint slide curve.
      6. "remove content/text/button on featured area" → featured is now IMAGE-ONLY. Removed eyebrow/
         title/description/count/CTA overlay. Intro slide keeps only the centred section title (it has no
         image). NOTE: text still visible ON the images (CAVERO / OUD COLLECTION etc.) is BAKED INTO the
         uploaded artwork — not HTML; can only be changed by re-uploading category images in admin.
      7. "images are cut / gone out of box" → REMOVED Ken Burns (it scaled to 1.14 + panned -2%, pushing
         artwork past the clipped box). Image now static within the box.
      8. "still show zoomed" → tried object-fit:contain (full image, dark matting) → owner said REVERT.
         Final state = object-fit:cover (full-bleed) on both featured + row images.
      9. "replace A Symphony of Fragrance → Combo + add combo image" → the rotating SET is now
         [Combos slide, ...categories] (was [Symphony intro, ...]). The "A Symphony of Fragrance"
         intro slide/tile is REMOVED entirely.
         · NEW asset: copied C:\Users\DELL\Downloads\IMG_3666.PNG → src/assets/images/combo-showcase.png
           (a bundled asset — angular.json maps src/assets → /assets, so served at
           assets/images/combo-showcase.png; NOT the backend /uploads path).
         · comboItem in featured-categories.component.ts: { type:'combo', id:'combo-showcase',
           title:'Combos', subtitle:'Curated Value Sets', image:'assets/images/combo-showcase.png',
           link:'/combo', ctaLabel:'Shop Combos' }. It is items[0] → featured on first paint.
         · ShowcaseItem.type is now 'combo' | 'category' (was 'intro' | 'category'); added a `subtitle`
           field. Category subtitle = getProductCountLabel(count) (precomputed at build); combo
           subtitle = "Curated Value Sets".
         · Rendering is now IMAGE-DRIVEN, not type-driven: featTpl + row card branch on `item.image`
           (every slide has an image now), so combo + categories render identically (full-bleed cover
           image; row tile = image + name + subtitle overlaid). The old intro/featIntro text-only
           branches remain only as empty-image fallbacks. Combo tile links to /combo.

      FINAL STATE: rotating set = [Combos, ...categories]; image-only featured card, full-bleed cover
      images, NO Ken Burns. Motion kept = slide, cross-fade, progress line, up-next glow, hover lift.
      Build zero errors (the "8 rules skipped" warning is pre-existing/unrelated). Verified via temp

    Session 42 COMPLETED (Alert/toast behaviour fix — owner report: alerts stayed too long + stacked, 2026-06-19):
    Owner screenshot showed FOUR identical "Stock Limit Reached" toasts piled up from tapping qty-up at a
    stock-capped item. Two requirements: (1) alerts must auto-dismiss in ~2s; (2) never show repeated copies of
    the same alert. Both fixed in ONE shared service so every alert site (cart, product detail, checkout,
    wishlist, etc.) inherits the behaviour.

      FILE: src/app/shared/services/frontend-notification.service.ts (the single service all alerts route through)
      • DURATION → 2000ms for EVERY variant. Was duration = (celebrate ? 4800 : 3800). Now one
        private DURATION = 2000 const used for success/error/info/celebrate alike; the shrinking progress bar
        animation is driven off the same value so it matches the 2s window.
      • DE-DUPE (the stacking fix): each toast is tagged data-key="${variant}|${message}". show() first queries
        the stack for a non-leaving toast with the same key — if found, it does NOT create a new toast; it calls
        that toast's _restartTimer() (resets remaining→2s, replays the progress bar) and returns. So spamming
        qty-up / add-to-cart refreshes ONE toast instead of piling new ones.
      • Refactored the auto-dismiss into a restartable startTimer() closure (hover-to-pause still works:
        mouseenter clears timer + pauses progress, mouseleave re-arms with remaining time). Exposed
        _restartTimer() + _clearTimer() on the toast element.
      • MAX_VISIBLE 4 → 3 (safety cap for genuinely DIFFERENT messages).
      • Added cssEscape() helper (native CSS.escape with a regex fallback) so messages containing quotes/brackets
        can't break the [data-key="…"] selector.
      • The reduced-motion / prefers-reduced-motion path also got a _restartTimer so de-dupe works there too.

      STALE TEST FIX (data drift, NOT a regression from this work):
      • npm run test:chrome → first run 79 passed / 1 failed. The fail was 04-catalog "loads 10 product cards":
        the storefront now renders 11 product cards (a product was added, likely during Session 41 admin QA), but
        the spec hard-coded toHaveCount(10). A toast service cannot change product counts — pure data drift.
      • FIX (tests/specs/04-catalog.spec.ts): replaced the brittle exact count with a resilient assertion — grid
        is non-empty (cardCount >= 10) AND cardCount === the catalog's own count-label (getProductCount()). Renamed
        test → "loads the product cards". Won't break again each time the owner adds a product.

      VERIFICATION:
      ✓ npm run build → Application bundle generation complete, zero errors (the "8 rules skipped / selector
        errors" CSS warning is pre-existing — Session 32's 4 harmless warnings; this change only touched TS).
      ✓ npm run test:chrome → 79 passed / 1 failed (the stale 04-catalog count) → after the spec fix,
        npx playwright test 04-catalog --project=chrome → 9/9 passed. Effective suite: 80/80 green.

      → Next: Cloudinary + Phase 6 deploy (unchanged). Carry-over cleanups unchanged from S41 (delete dead
        profile.component.html; resolve Lordicon license; remove .theme-backup-src; full 4-browser Playwright run).


    Session 43 COMPLETED (Admin sidebar white labels + NEW Logo settings module, 2026-06-24):
      Two owner requests in one session. Both Playwright-verified, build green throughout.

      A) ADMIN SIDEBAR MENU LABELS → WHITE (readability bug):
        Owner screenshot: every inactive nav label (Dashboard, Inventory, Variant Types, Categories, Combos,
        Orders, Returns, Coupons, Free Gifts) rendered dark-on-dark — only the active "Products" pill was legible.
        ROOT CAUSE (not the obvious one): a GLOBAL storefront light-theme rule in src/assets/scss/style.scss:297
          html:not([data-bs-theme="dark"]) a:not(.btn):not([class*="-btn"]) { color:#1A322D !important; }
        forces EVERY anchor dark-green with !important. The admin shell renders under the light theme, and the
        sidebar items are <a class="cv-nav-item"> → they inherited #1A322D (dark-green-on-dark). The icons are
        <span> (not anchors) so they escaped the rule — which is why only icons looked right at first. The earlier
        _atelier.scss colour edits could not win because of the storefront rule's !important.
        FIX (mirrors the EXISTING .cavero-footer carve-out a few lines above): added a dedicated exemption in
        style.scss for the admin shell sidebar links —
          html:not([data-bs-theme="dark"]) .cv-shell .cv-nav-item:not(.is-active) { color:#FFFFFF !important; }
        Specificity (0,3,1)!important beats the global (0,2,2)!important, and :not(.is-active) leaves the active
        item's dark-on-mint styling untouched. Also brightened in src/assets/scss/_atelier.scss: .cv-nav-item label
        → #FFFFFF, icons → rgba(255,255,255,.85), group headers (OVERVIEW/CATALOG/SALES) → mint rgba(159,227,197,.72).
        VERIFY: temp Playwright spec logged into admin, read getComputedStyle on an inactive .cv-nav-item →
        rgb(255,255,255) ✓ (was rgb(26,50,45)=#1A322D); screenshot confirmed all labels readable white. The 3
        admin-dialog test "failures" during the run were the LOGIN RATE LIMITER (08-admin's deliberate rate-limit
        test runs first and starves later specs) — re-running 10-admin-dialogs alone = 3/3 passed. No regression.
        NOTE for future "admin text is dark-on-dark" bugs: check this storefront anchor rule FIRST — the admin shell
        lives under the light theme, so any <a> in admin needs a .cv-shell carve-out (footer + sidebar now have one).

        Owner asked for a Logo page under Settings to manage the site logo, with instructions stating the required
        size/pixels are mandatory for best visibility. Built end-to-end following the Contact-Settings + Sliders
        patterns (settings stored as JSON in cms_page; image uploaded via multer to /assets/uploads, served at

          · GET  / → reads cms_page slug 'logo-settings', returns { logo_url } ('' = use bundled default).
          · POST / (multer upload.single('logo'), 2MB cap, png/jpg/webp/svg) → saves to /assets/uploads/logo/,

        .cavero-auth-card anchor carve-out in style.scss; signup hero differentiated (eyebrow "Join Cavero" + perks).

      CHECKOUT / CART (critical):
      • ₹1 CHARGE BUG: checkout used cart.total which read 0 at pay time → Math.max(1,…) clamped to ₹1, and saveOrder
        recorded total_amount=₹1 too. FIX: computePayable() derives subtotal/discount/delivery/total from the live
        cart ITEMS for BOTH the Razorpay amount and the saved order; aborts (guard) on empty cart.
      • New-customer address: checkout now shows an explicit "Add a delivery address" heading + a "Save this address"
        checkbox → on Place Order it POSTs to /api/addresses (first address becomes default). Verified saved.
      • COUPON lost cart→checkout: fetchServerCart() now preserves the client-applied coupon (applied_coupon/code/id)
        across the server-cart refresh; syncCouponState recomputes the discount. Verified on order #29.

      ACCOUNT ROUTE CONSOLIDATION (old → /profile):
      • app.routes.ts: /my-orders, /wishlist, /payment-methods, /my-tickets now redirectTo 'profile' (old separate
        account pages removed from routing; their component imports dropped — files remain as dead code).
      • order-success "View My Orders" → routerLink /profile fragment 'orders'. Navbar already used /profile +
        /profile#wishlist. profile.component uses an INLINE template (the .html file is dead).
      • Support Tickets MOVED into a new /profile#support section (sidebar item + count badge): create form (subject +
        message) + ticket list with status badges + empty state. Reads /api/support-tickets/user, posts /api/support-tickets.

      SUPPORT TICKET SUBMIT FIX (backend):
      • supportTicketRoutes.js POST awaited the Gmail notification BEFORE creating the ticket, so any mail failure
        500'd the request and nothing saved. Reordered: create the ticket FIRST, send email fire-and-forget (.catch).
        Admin Support Tickets date column widened + shown as 2-line date/time (was wrapping badly).

    Session 45 COMPLETED (Production-hardening pass — security, payment integrity, deploy prep, 2026-06-26..28):
      Trigger: owner asked "is my project production-ready / any bugs / security issues?" Did a deep audit then fixed
      the criticals. Backend = plain node (no nodemon): RESTART after every change (done throughout).

      AUDIT — headline findings:
        • CRITICAL price/total tampering: server trusted client-sent item prices + subtotal (and the Razorpay amount
          was client-computed) → a ₹2000 order could be bought for ₹1 (COD and online). FIXED (see below).
        • Many admin/write endpoints had NO auth guard. FIXED.
        • Orders created AFTER payment (client-side) → dropped connection = paid-but-no-order. FIXED via #2.
        • Good already: coupon re-validated + discount recomputed server-side; stock reserved in a txn w/ row locks;
          CORS allowlist; Helmet; password reset w/ hashed tokens; login rate-limit.

      (A) API AUTHORIZATION — added verifyToken+verifySuperuser to all admin writes:
        categoryRoutes / brandRoutes / sliderRoutes (POST/PUT/DELETE/bulk-delete); supportTicketRoutes (admin GET '/'
        + PUT /:id/status; POST '/' stays PUBLIC for the contact form); contactSettings/logoSettings(+DELETE)/
        aboutSettings/comboBoxSettings POST; authRoutes coupon CRUD + GET /coupons + /coupons/:id/usages.
        addressRoutes: EVERY route → verifyToken + isOwnerOrAdmin ownership (was an IDOR — anyone could read/edit any
        user's addresses by user_id); POST forces user_id from the token. authRoutes.js had a SECOND local verifyToken
        (signature-only) — upgraded it to the same deleted/inactive-user DB check as middleware/auth.js so the gap is
        closed everywhere. Verified: public reads 200, unauth writes/admin/address 403, admin-with-token 200.

      (B) PRICE/TOTAL INTEGRITY (the critical fix):
        utils/orderInventory.js → new priceOrderItemsFromDb(): recomputes each line price from the DB (variant.price
        else product.price) + the trusted subtotal. COMBO lines keep their allocated price (checkout tags them with
        combo_id; normalizeOrderItems carries combo_id; the pricer skips them) — combo logic untouched.
        orderRoutes POST now uses the SERVER subtotal/total/item-prices (ignores client subtotal/prices). For ONLINE it
        fetches the Razorpay order and rejects if its amount != server total (blocks underpayment). Verified: a tampered
        ₹1 order was saved as the real ₹399. Legit orders unchanged.

      (C) #2 CREATE-PENDING-ORDER-BEFORE-PAY + reserve + auto-cleanup:
        Frontend razorpayService split → createRazorpayOrder() + public openCheckout(); checkout.processOnlinePayment
        now: createRazorpayOrder → POST our order as PENDING (stock reserved, amount validated) → openCheckout → verify
        marks paid → navigate. On cancel/failure it calls POST /api/orders/:id/cancel (NEW, ownership-checked) which
        restores stock + reverses coupon + marks cancelled/failed. SAFETY NET utils/pendingOrders.js
        cleanupStalePendingOrders() scheduled in server.js (~60s after boot, then every 15min): finds online orders
        'pending' >45min, DOUBLE-CHECKS Razorpay → reconciles to paid if actually paid, else releases (so a paid order
        is never wrongly cancelled even without a webhook). Dup-guard added by razorpay_order_id. FIXED a real bug:
        restoreInventoryForOrderItems restored only PRODUCT stock — now restores VARIANT + JSONB sizes too (no leak on
        cancel/refund). Verified: cancel restored variant+product stock; backend boots; full online path needs a real
        Razorpay payment to test in the UI.

      (D) RAZORPAY WEBHOOK: POST /api/payment/webhook — verifies x-razorpay-signature (RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET; raw
        body captured via bodyParser verify in server.js), idempotent, on payment.captured/order.paid marks the
        matching order paid, logs orphan payments. Tested (valid/known/orphan/invalid-sig + pending→paid flip).
        ⚠ set RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET (+ Dashboard webhook URL) when live.

      (E) GOOGLE OAUTH rebrand: new client id 408429788067-... in environment.ts + environment.development.ts
        (googleClientId) + .env (GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID + GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET). ⚠ NEEDS Google Console: consent-screen App
        name "Cavero Fragrances" + Authorized JS origin https://caverofragrance.com (current error w/o it:
        "no registered origin / invalid_client"). The "yuvic" popup text = consent-screen App name, not code.

      (F) DOMAIN corrected caverofragrances.in → caverofragrance.com in invoice.service.ts, packing slip
        (allorders.component.ts), reel placeholder, robots.txt, sitemap.xml. (sitemap still has stale category slugs —
        regenerate before launch.)

      (G) Removed verbose debug console.logs from orderRoutes.js + orderInventory.js (kept error/warn + 1 email-sent info).

      STILL OPEN before production launch:
        1. Cloudinary (uploads on local disk → wiped on cloud restart — DEPLOY BLOCKER; need CLOUD_NAME/API_KEY/SECRET).
        2. Deploy: managed Postgres (replace DB_PASSWORD=123), backend (Railway/Render) + frontend (Vercel), new strong

  [DONE]  4.7c  Production Variants Module — dedicated DB table (Session 10)
                NEW TABLE: product_variants
                  id, product_id (FK→products CASCADE DELETE), name VARCHAR(100),
                  sku VARCHAR(100), price DECIMAL(10,2), compare_price DECIMAL(10,2),
                  stock INTEGER, low_stock_threshold INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
                  is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true, sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0

                NEW FILES:
                  backend/auth-service/models/product_variant.js
                    — Sequelize model; sets up Product.hasMany(ProductVariant, as:'variants')
                  backend/auth-service/routes/variantRoutes.js
                    — REST CRUD at /api/variants (public GET, admin POST/PUT/DELETE/reorder)

                BACKEND CHANGES:
                  migrate.js          — CREATE TABLE product_variants + ADD variant_id to order_items
                  server.js           — require product_variant model, mount /api/variants
                  productRoutes.js    — syncVariants() helper; GET /products includes variants[];
                                        POST/PUT parse 'variants' JSON and call syncVariants after save
                  order_item.js       — variant_id INTEGER field added
                  orderRoutes.js      — saves variant_id per order item in bulkCreate
                  orderInventory.js   — normalizes variant_id; deducts product_variants.stock
                                        from DB table; keeps JSONB fallback for old orders

                FRONTEND CHANGES:
                  cart.model.ts                 — variant_id?: number added to CartItem
                  cart.service.ts               — addToCart accepts variant_id; resolves variant
                                                  price from product.variants[]; matches by variant_id
                  product-detail.service.ts     — variants[] array added to Product interface
                  product-detail.component.ts   — passes variant_id to cartService.addToCart
                  product-detail-info.ts        — getVariants() reads product.variants[] first,
                                                  falls back to specifications.sizes JSONB for old
                                                  products; emits variant_id in addToCartEvent
                  product-detail-info.html      — uses getVariants(), v.name (not v.label)
                  checkout.component.ts         — buildOrderItems includes variant_id per line item
                  form-dialog.component.ts      — full VariantRow[] model, addVariant(),
                                                  removeVariant(), onVariantNameChange(),
                                                  generateVariantSku(); loads from product.variants[]
                                                  on edit; legacy JSONB migration on first edit
                  form-dialog.component.html    — FULL DYNAMIC MODULE replacing old Size Variants:
                                                  empty state / column headers / rows with:
                                                  Name, SKU, Price(₹), MRP(₹), Stock, Low-Stock-Alert,
                                                  Active slide-toggle, Delete button

                HOW IT WORKS END-TO-END:
                  1. Admin creates product → adds variants (30ml ₹499, 50ml ₹799, 100ml ₹1299)
                     each with own SKU, price, MRP, stock, alert threshold, active flag
                  2. Customer opens product page → size buttons rendered from product.variants[]
                     Active=false variants hidden; stock=0 variants shown as "Sold Out" (disabled)
                  3. Customer MUST select a size before "Add to cart" activates
                  4. Cart stores variant_id + variant name "(50ml)" suffix; price from variant
                  5. On order placement → product.stock decremented AND product_variants.stock
                     decremented via DB UPDATE (not JSONB patch)
                  6. order_items row records variant_id + selected_size for admin reports
                  7. When variant stock hits 0 → "Sold Out" on next page load

                BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:
                  Old products that have specifications.sizes JSONB still display correctly.
                  product-detail-info reads product.variants[] first; if empty, reads JSONB.
                  orderInventory deducts JSONB sizes only when variant_id is absent.

  MULTI-CATEGORY SYSTEM (Session 12):
    Each product has category_id (primary FK) AND specifications.categories[] (JSONB array of slugs).
    productRoutes.js categoryproducts endpoint matches:

    Result: browsing /shop-category/oud-collection shows 3 products (Ameer Al Oudh, White Oudh,
            Oud & Roses), browsing /shop-category/unisex-fragrances shows 5 products.

====================================================
SECTION 8 : ARCHITECTURE NOTES
====================================================

  PERFUME FIELDS
    Concentration, Family, Gender stored in specifications JSONB column.
    No DB schema change needed for new perfume attributes.
    Access in templates: product?.specifications?.concentration

  VARIANTS MODULE (Session 10 — Phase 4.7c)  ← CURRENT SYSTEM
    Storage: dedicated product_variants table (relational FK, not JSONB).
    Each variant: id, product_id, name, sku, price, compare_price, stock,
                  low_stock_threshold, is_active, sort_order.

    ADMIN FLOW (form-dialog.component.ts):
      - "Add Variant" button adds a row dynamically (sort_order = array index)
      - Per row: Name (e.g. "50ml"), SKU (auto-generated from product SKU + name),
        Price(₹), MRP(₹), Stock, Low-Stock-Alert, Active slide-toggle, Delete
      - is_active=false hides variant from customers without deleting it
      - On form submit: variants sent as JSON field in FormData
      - Backend syncVariants() diffs submitted list vs DB: creates new, updates existing,
        deletes removed
      - On edit: existing variants loaded from product.variants[] array in API response

    CUSTOMER FLOW (product-detail-info.component.ts):
      - getVariants() reads product.variants[] (sorted by sort_order, filtered is_active)
      - Falls back to specifications.sizes JSONB for old products (backward compat)
      - Size buttons rendered; stock=0 → "Sold Out" disabled button
      - Must select a variant before "Add to cart" if variants exist
      - Selected variant price shown; variant_id + selected_size both emitted

    CART + CHECKOUT:
      - CartItem stores variant_id + selected_size + uses variant.price (not product.price)
      - buildOrderItems passes variant_id per line item to order API

    INVENTORY:
      - On order placement: product.stock decremented (parent) AND product_variants.stock
        decremented via DB UPDATE (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE lock-safe)
      - JSONB-based deduction kept as fallback for items with selected_size but no variant_id

    IF NO VARIANTS: product sells as single SKU using base product price/stock. No size
    selector shown. Backward compatible — existing products unaffected.

  SIZE VARIANTS JSONB (Sessions 5-9 — LEGACY / backward compat only)
    Old approach: specifications.sizes = [{ label, price, compare_price, stock }, ...]
    Still read by product-detail-info.component.ts as fallback if product.variants[] is empty.
    New products should use the product_variants table via the admin form.
    Do NOT add new sizes via JSONB; use the admin Variants section instead.

  FRAGRANCE FILTERS (Session 5 — Phase 4.3)
    Catalog sidebar: Concentration, Fragrance Family, Gender filter groups.
    Values come from product.specifications JSONB — exact string match.
    ActiveFilters interface: { priceRange, status, labels, concentration[], fragranceFamily[], gender[] }
    Filtering is fully client-side (200 products pre-loaded).
    Filter count badge (gold pill) shows selected count per group.
    Labels array drives the active filter chips in the product grid area.

  FREE GIFTS (Session 5 — Phase 4.5)
    GET /api/free-gifts/active returns gifts sorted ascending by min_order_value.
    Cart checks subtotal against thresholds — shows progress bar and unlocked banners.
    Admin can manage gifts at /api/free-gifts (full CRUD, token required).
    Gifts currently live: ₹999 → Attar Sample, ₹1999 → Oud Incense Stick.

  ANGULAR.JSON FIX (Session 5)
    styles[] had: src/assets/yuvic/vendor/swiper/swiper-bundle.min.css
    scripts[] had: src/assets/yuvic/vendor/swiper/swiper-bundle.min.js
                   src/assets/yuvic/js/theme.min.js
    All updated to src/assets/cavero/... paths. Build now zero errors.

  THEME / CSS
    Base CSS: src/assets/cavero/css/ (renamed Session 4 — Phase 5.6 complete)
    Gold overrides in src/styles.scss via CSS custom properties:
      --cz-primary: #C9A84C
    All component selectors: app-cavero-*, all imports: themes/cavero/

  LOGO
    SVG at src/assets/images/cavero-logo.svg (text wordmark)
    Has onerror fallback: renders "CAVERO FRAGRANCES" as styled HTML
    Replace with proper designed logo when ready (Phase 4.8).

  AUTH FLOW
    Admin    : POST /api/auth/login   → requires superuser/admin role, password-only
    Customer : POST /api/auth/signin  → blocks superuser/admin roles, password-only
    Google   : POST /api/auth/google-signin → uses verifyIdToken (secure)
    Guard    : auth.guard.ts uses .toLowerCase() for role comparison
    Interceptor: error.interceptor.ts — auto-logout on 401 EXCEPT /login /signin
    Token key: localStorage 'authToken' (used by auth.service.ts + Playwright tests)
    OTP login: REMOVED in Session 8 — /send-otp endpoint deleted, no OTP UI anywhere

  LOGIN ERROR HANDLING (fixed Session 3)
    Admin signin now shows specific messages:
      status 0   → "Cannot reach server. Please ensure the backend is running."
      status 401 → "Invalid email or password."
      status 403 → "Access denied."
      status 429 → "Too many login attempts. Please wait a minute…"

  ROUTE FACTS (discovered in QA)
    Brands  : /api/brands  (NOT /api/auth/brands)
    CMS pub : /api/auth/cms/:slug
    CMS adm : /api/auth/cms-pages (requires admin token)
    Search  : /api/auth/categoryproducts?search=X (NOT /api/auth/products)
    Orders  : /api/orders
    Payment : /api/payment/create-order

  DB AUTO-SYNC
    Sequelize sync() runs on every server start → tables auto-created.
    migrate.js runs ALTER TABLE for specific column additions.

  CART
    Frontend uses localStorage only. cart DB table unused — ignore it.

  CONTACT SETTINGS
    Stored as CMS page, slug='contact-settings'.
    Content is JSON: {email1, email2, phone, address, seo_title, ...}

  DOTENV LOADING ORDER (critical — fixed Session 3)
    server.js MUST load dotenv before requiring sequelize/config/db.
    If dotenv loads after, DB_USER is undefined and pg falls back to
    the Windows OS username (e.g. "DELL"), causing auth failure.
    Current order in server.js:
      Line 1: const path = require('path')
      Line 2: const dotenv = require('dotenv')
      Line 3: dotenv.config({ path: path.join(__dirname, '.env') })  ← FIRST
      Line 5: const sequelize = require('./config/db')               ← AFTER


====================================================
SECTION 9 : SEED SCRIPTS REFERENCE
====================================================

  Run if cavero_db is wiped or you set up on a new machine:

  Step 1 — Create the database:
    psql -U postgres -c "CREATE DATABASE cavero_db ENCODING 'UTF8';"

  Step 2 — Start server once to auto-create all 19 tables:
    cd backend/auth-service
    node server.js
    (wait for "Auth service running on port 5000", then Ctrl+C)

  Step 3 — Seed base data (roles, admin, categories, brands):
    node backend/auth-service/scripts/seed.js

  Step 4 — Seed products and CMS pages:
    node backend/auth-service/scripts/seed-products-cms.js

  After seeding, run npm run dev to start normally.

  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  CATEGORY A : CRITICAL BUGS  (ALL FIXED ✓)
  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  [BUG-01] ★ FIXED — OTP VALUE LEAKED IN API RESPONSE
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js  line 716
    Code   : return res.json({ success: true, otp, message: "OTP sent successfully" });
    Impact : The raw OTP number is returned in the HTTP response body.
             An attacker can sign up, call /send-otp, read the response,
             and log in without ever checking their email. OTP is useless.
    Fix    : Remove the `otp` field from the response:
             return res.json({ success: true, message: "OTP sent to your email." });
    Effort : 1 line

  [BUG-02] ★ FIXED — FAVICON REBRANDED
    File   : src/index.html  line 16
    Code   : <link rel="icon" href="assets/images/yuvik_favicon.png" type="image/x-icon">
    Impact : Every browser tab and bookmark shows the OLD Yuvic brand icon.
             Customers will see "YuViK" icon on Cavero's website.
    Fix    : Create a Cavero favicon (gold "C" or full icon).
             Until then, replace with cavero-logo.svg or remove to show browser default.
             Change line 16 to: <link rel="icon" href="assets/images/cavero-logo.svg" type="image/svg+xml">
    Effort : 1 line change + optional favicon file creation

  [BUG-03] ★ FIXED — HARDCODED localhost:5000 REMOVED FROM ADMIN COMPONENTS
    Files  : src/app/siteadmin/about-editor/about-editor.component.ts  line 14
             src/app/siteadmin/terms-editor/terms-editor.component.ts  line 14
    Code   : const API = 'http://localhost:5000/api/about-settings';
             const API = 'http://localhost:5000/api/auth';
    Impact : About page editor and Terms editor will silently fail in production.
             Admin will see errors when trying to edit About/Terms content after deployment.
    Fix    : Replace hardcoded URLs with environment imports:
             import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';
             const API = `${environment.apiGatewayBaseUrl}/api/about-settings`;
    Effort : ~10 lines per file

  [BUG-04] ★ FIXED — FREE GIFTS ADMIN COMPONENT BUILT + WIRED
    File   : src/app/siteadmin/siteadmin.routes.ts  lines 55-58
    Code   : path: 'freegifts', loadComponent: () => import('./categories/allcategories/allcategories.component')
    Impact : /siteadmin/freegifts shows the Categories page, not Free Gifts management.
             Admin cannot manage free gifts (add/remove/toggle) from the UI.
             Free gifts can only be managed via raw API calls.
    Fix    : Build a FreeGiftsComponent admin page OR add Free Gifts management
             to the existing admin dashboard. A minimal CRUD table would take ~2 hours.
    Effort : Medium (~2 hours to build component)

  [BUG-05] ★ FIXED — PRODUCTION environment.ts RAZORPAY KEY CLEARED
    File   : src/environments/environment.ts  line 28
    Code   : razorpayKey: 'rzp_test_SdknSR5NK48L65'
    Impact : When npm run build compiles for production, the Angular app will use the
             TEST Razorpay key. In live mode, the server sends the live key from .env
             via /api/payment/config — but if that API call fails, the frontend falls
             back to environment.razorpayKey which is the TEST key.
             Result: payment modal opens in TEST mode on live site.
    Fix    : Either:
             A) Set razorpayKey: '' in environment.ts (safest — forces server config)
             B) Set to live key: razorpayKey: 'rzp_live_SyQX1m5kVuNTRe'
             (Option A recommended — the backend drives the key, not the frontend)
    Effort : 1 line change

  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  CATEGORY B : SECURITY ISSUES  (most fixed ✓, 3 pending for production)
  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  [SEC-01] ★ FIXED — JWT FALLBACK 'your-secret-key' REMOVED FROM ALL ROUTE FILES
    Files  : freeGiftRoutes.js:11, returnRoutes.js:13, reviewRoutes.js:16,
             supportTicketRoutes.js:12, orderRoutes.js:129
    Code   : jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'your-secret-key', ...)
    Risk   : If JWT_SECRET is ever undefined at runtime (bad .env deploy), these
             5 routes will verify tokens with the known weak secret.
    Fix    : Replace all 5 duplicate verifyToken implementations by importing
             the shared middleware:
             const { verifyToken, verifySuperuser } = require('../middleware/auth');
             (middleware/auth.js already has the correct implementation without fallback)
    Effort : ~5 minutes — find/replace in each file

  [SEC-02] ★ FIXED — /api/payment/create-order NOW REQUIRES AUTH (HTTP 403 without token)
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/paymentRoutes.js  line 60
    Risk   : Any anonymous user or bot can spam Razorpay order creation.
             Could exhaust Razorpay API limits or be used for reconnaissance.
    Fix    : Add verifyToken middleware:
             router.post('/create-order', verifyToken, async (req, res) => { ... })
    Effort : 1 line

  [SEC-03] ★ FIXED — helmet.js ADDED TO server.js (X-Frame-Options, HSTS, etc.)
    File   : backend/auth-service/server.js
    Missing: X-Frame-Options (clickjacking), Content-Security-Policy,
             X-Content-Type-Options, Strict-Transport-Security (HSTS),
             X-XSS-Protection
    Fix    : npm install helmet --save  (in backend/auth-service/)
             Add near top of server.js:
               const helmet = require('helmet');
               app.use(helmet());
    Effort : 5 minutes

  [SEC-04] ○ PENDING — DATABASE PASSWORD IS '123' — CHANGE IN PRODUCTION .env
    File   : backend/auth-service/.env  DB_PASSWORD=123
    Risk   : Trivially guessable password. On Railway/production DB, this MUST be
             a strong random password.
    Fix    : Before deploying, generate a strong password for the production DB.
             This is part of Phase 6.4 production .env creation.
    Effort : 5 minutes (change password in DB + .env)

  [SEC-05] ★ FIXED — OTP UPGRADED TO 6 DIGITS + 10-MINUTE EXPIRY
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js  line 696
    Code   : const otp = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
    Risk   : With rate limiting at 10/min and 9000 possible values, an attacker
             needs ~15 minutes to brute-force an OTP. Low risk but suboptimal.
             Also: OTP expires in 1 minute (line 701) — very short, may frustrate users
             if email delivery is slow (common with Gmail).
    Fix    : Use 6-digit OTP: Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)
             Extend expiry to 10 minutes: Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000
    Effort : 2 lines

  [SEC-06] ○ PENDING — FRONTEND_URL NOT SET IN .env — add to production .env
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/referralRoutes.js  line 33
    Code   : const baseUrl = process.env.FRONTEND_URL || 'http://localhost:4200';
    Risk   : WhatsApp referral share link will contain localhost URL in production.
    Fix    : Add FRONTEND_URL=https://yourdomain.com to production .env
    Effort : 1 line in .env

  [SEC-07] CMS [innerHTML] — ANGULAR DEFAULT SANITIZATION ONLY
    File   : src/app/landing/cms/cms.component.html  line 57
    Code   : <div class="cms-content" [innerHTML]="pageContent.content"></div>
    Note   : Angular's DomSanitizer automatically sanitizes [innerHTML] bindings
             by default. This is SAFE as long as no bypassSecurityTrustHtml() is used.
             Current code does NOT bypass — no immediate action needed.
             But: if a future developer adds bypassSecurityTrustHtml to render CKEditor
             HTML, it would become XSS-vulnerable. Document this for the team.
    Action : Add a comment above the binding warning future developers.

    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/reviewRoutes.js  line 37
    Code   : // Check if user already reviewed this product? (Optional, skipping for now)
    Impact : A customer can submit 10 reviews for the same product, inflating ratings.
    Fix    : Add unique constraint check before Review.create():
             const existing = await Review.findOne({ where: { user_id, product_id } });
             if (existing) return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Already reviewed' });

  [MISS-05] ★ FIXED — CHECKOUT PHONE (Indian mobile) + PINCODE (6-digit) VALIDATED
    File   : src/app/landing/checkout/checkout.component.ts  lines 39+42
    Code   : phone: ['', Validators.required]   ← no pattern
             zipCode: ['', Validators.required]  ← no pattern
    Impact : Customer can enter "abc" as phone or "123" as pincode, causing order
             delivery failures without clear error messages.
    Fix    : phone: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern(/^[6-9]\d{9}$/)]]
             zipCode: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern(/^\d{6}$/)]]
    Effort : 2 lines

  [CODE-01] ★ FIXED — verifyToken DUPLICATION REMOVED (all 5 files → middleware/auth.js)
    Files  : freeGiftRoutes.js, returnRoutes.js, reviewRoutes.js,

             const { verifyToken, verifySuperuser } = require('../middleware/auth');

  [CODE-02] generateUniqueReferralCode DUPLICATED
    Files  : authRoutes.js:49-60  AND  referralRoutes.js:8-18

  [CODE-03] 196 console.log STATEMENTS IN BACKEND ROUTES
    These are development debug artifacts. In production, logs should be structured
    (use winston or pino) and sensitive data (OTP, user IDs) should not be logged.
    Particularly: authRoutes.js:697 — console.log("otp", otp) — logs OTP to console.
    Minimum fix: Remove the OTP console.log.
    Full fix: Replace console.log with structured logger.

  [CODE-04] MULTER IMAGES SAVED TO src/assets/uploads/ — NOT CLOUD-SAFE
    See Phase 6.7 — all uploaded images are on local disk.
    Cloud deployments (Railway, Render, Fly.io) have ephemeral filesystems.
    Every server restart will wipe uploaded product images, slider images, etc.
    Fix: Cloudinary (free tier) + multer-storage-cloudinary is the easiest path.
    This is a DEPLOYMENT BLOCKER.

  10W. SESSION 10 WORK — PRODUCTION VARIANTS MODULE (2026-06-10)

       ARCHITECTURE DECISION:
       Replaced specifications.sizes JSONB approach (Session 9) with a proper
       relational product_variants table. Reasons:
         - Variants can be queried individually by ID (needed for order items FK)
         - Stock deduction is a simple UPDATE on a row — no JSONB patch needed
         - Admin can add/edit/delete variants independently via /api/variants
         - is_active flag allows hiding variants without stock = 0 (e.g. seasonal)
         - sort_order allows controlling display sequence

       NEW FILES:
       [x] backend/auth-service/models/product_variant.js
             Sequelize model for product_variants table.
             Sets up Product.hasMany(ProductVariant, as:'variants', onDelete:'CASCADE')
             and ProductVariant.belongsTo(Product).
       [x] backend/auth-service/routes/variantRoutes.js
             REST CRUD mounted at /api/variants.
             Public: GET /product/:id (active variants)
             Admin:  GET /product/:id/all, POST, PUT/:id, DELETE/:id, PATCH/reorder

       BACKEND CHANGES:
       [x] migrate.js
             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product_variants (full schema with FK)
             CREATE INDEX idx_product_variants_product_id
             ALTER TABLE order_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS variant_id INTEGER
       [x] server.js
             require('./models/product_variant') — registers Product.hasMany association
             variantRoutes mounted at /api/variants
       [x] routes/productRoutes.js
             require ProductVariant at top
             syncVariants(productId, variantsData) helper — full create/update/delete diff
             formatVariantRow() helper for consistent API response shape
             GET /products: include ProductVariant as 'variants' (separate:true, sorted)
             formattedProducts includes variants: [] in every response
             POST /products: parses 'variants' from form body, calls syncVariants after create
             PUT /products/:id: parses 'variants' from form body, calls syncVariants after update
       [x] models/order_item.js
             variant_id: DataTypes.INTEGER, allowNull:true, defaultValue:null
       [x] routes/orderRoutes.js
             orderItemsData.map includes variant_id: item.variant_id || null
       [x] utils/orderInventory.js
             require ProductVariant at top
             normalizeOrderItems: extracts variant_id from each item
             reserveInventoryForOrderItems: new block deducts product_variants.stock via
               DB UPDATE (with transaction lock) when item.variant_id is set
             Kept legacy JSONB deduction as fallback for items without variant_id

       FRONTEND CHANGES:
       [x] src/app/landing/models/cart.model.ts
             CartItem.variant_id?: number added
       [x] src/app/landing/cart/cart.service.ts
             addToCart signature: (product, qty, showToast, selected_size?, variant_id?)
             Price resolution: if variant_id present, look up variant in product.variants[]
             Cart item matching: by variant_id when both sides have it, else product+size
             CartItem stored with variant_id field
       [x] src/app/landing/product-detail/product-detail.service.ts
             Product.variants?: Array<{id, name, sku, price, compare_price, stock,
             low_stock_threshold, is_active, sort_order}> added to interface
       [x] src/app/landing/product-detail/product-detail.component.ts
             addToCart(event) now passes event.variant_id to cartService.addToCart
       [x] src/app/landing/product-detail/section/info/product-detail-info.component.ts
             getVariants() — reads product.variants[] (sorted, filtered active),
               falls back to specifications.sizes JSONB for old products
             getSizeVariants() kept as alias for backward compat
             getSelectedVariant() returns object with both .name and .label fields
             getDisplayComparePrice() fixed for nullable compare_price (Number() cast)
             addToCart() emits {product, quantity, selected_size, variant_id}
             addToCartEvent type updated to include variant_id?: number
       [x] src/app/landing/product-detail/section/info/product-detail-info.component.html
             getSizeVariants() → getVariants() in template

           Old: this.id = slider.id || this.getRandomID()  ← generated fake UUID for every new slider

           Effect: POST body no longer includes a random fake id field

           — isUploading = true + cdr.detectChanges() set FIRST (before any async) → no race condition
           — id field skipped in FormData for 'add' action

           — console.log debug statements removed

         To REPLACE a banner image:

           (uses POST /api/auth/sliders — creates new DB record)

  10AA. SESSION 14 WORK — CMS PAGES WIRED + STATUS BUG FIXED (2026-06-11)

       CMS PAGES ADMIN — ROUTE + SIDEBAR WIRED:
       [x] src/app/siteadmin/siteadmin.routes.ts
             Added route: path 'cms-pages' → loadComponent AllCmsPageComponent
             (Component existed but had no route — was inaccessible)
       [x] src/assets/data/routes.json
             Added sidebar entry: "CMS Pages" with icon "article" between Sliders and Settings
             Role: Superuser only
       [x] Angular build: zero errors ✓ — allcmspage.component lazy chunk confirmed built

       CMS STATUS BUG — ROOT CAUSE + FULL FIX:
       ROOT CAUSE:
         allcmspage.component.ts loadData() converts status boolean → string for table display:
           status: row.status ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'
         When editCall(row) opens the dialog, it passes this row — status is now "Active" string.
         form-dialog.component.ts createCmsForm() binds this "Active" string to the mat-select.
         mat-select has [value]="true" and [value]="false" — "Active" doesn't match either.
         submit() sends status: "Active" to backend.
         PostgreSQL boolean column rejects string "Active" → SequelizeDatabaseError 22P02.

       FIX 1 — Frontend form-dialog.component.ts (createCmsForm):
         const rawStatus = this.cmsPage.status;
         const statusBool = rawStatus === 'Active' ? true :
                            rawStatus === 'Inactive' ? false :
                            rawStatus !== undefined ? Boolean(rawStatus) : true;
         Form control now always initializes with real boolean.

       FIX 2 — Frontend form-dialog.component.ts (submit):
         Explicitly casts status before sending:
           status: rawSt === true || rawSt === 'true' || rawSt === 'Active' ? true : false
         Ensures backend always receives a real boolean regardless of form state.

       FIX 3 — Backend authRoutes.js POST /cms-pages:
         const body = { ...req.body };
         body.status = body.status === true || body.status === 'true' ||
                       body.status === 'Active' || body.status === 1 || body.status === '1';
         CMSPage.create(body) — safe for any input.

       FIX 4 — Backend authRoutes.js PUT /cms-pages/:id:
         Same normalization before page.update(body).
         Defense-in-depth — even if frontend sends wrong type, backend corrects it.

       KEY FILES CHANGED SESSION 14:
         src/app/siteadmin/siteadmin.routes.ts                              ← NEW: cms-pages route added
         src/assets/data/routes.json                                         ← NEW: CMS Pages sidebar entry
         src/app/siteadmin/cmspage/allcmspage/dialogs/form-dialog/
           form-dialog.component.ts                                          ← FIXED: status boolean normalization
                                                                               in createCmsForm() + submit()
         backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js                          ← FIXED: status normalization in
                                                                               POST /cms-pages + PUT /cms-pages/:id

  10AC. SESSION 16 WORK — SECURITY QA AUDIT + HARDENING (2026-06-11)
====================================================

  ADMIN DATA ENTRY COMPLETED:
  [x]  Brands          — SKIPPED by owner decision (not required for launch)
  [x]  Influencer Reels — 3 reels added via /siteadmin/influencer-reels
         Reel 1: White Oudh — Light Arabian Oud (product: /product/white-oudh, sort: 1)

         Verified visible on customer-facing frontend ✓

         CAVERO10  — 10% off, min ₹500, max ₹200 off, 500 uses, valid till 2026-09-30
         WELCOME15 — 15% off, min ₹999, max ₹300 off, 1000 uses, valid till 2026-12-31
         FLAT100   — ₹100 flat off, min ₹799, 200 uses, valid till 2026-08-31
  [x]  Contact Settings — verified at /siteadmin/contact-settings (all fields correct)

  SECURITY HARDENING — 8 BUGS FOUND AND FIXED:

  [SEC-FIX-01] ★ bodyParser.json() had no size limit → large payload attack possible
    File   : backend/auth-service/server.js
    Fix    : app.use(bodyParser.json({ limit: '10kb' }))
             app.use(bodyParser.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '10kb' }))
    Impact : Blocks oversized payload/DoS attacks

  [SEC-FIX-02] ★ CSP (Content Security Policy) was disabled entirely
    File   : backend/auth-service/server.js
    Fix    : Re-enabled helmet CSP with Angular-safe directives:
             defaultSrc:'self', scriptSrc:'self'+'unsafe-inline',
             styleSrc:'self'+'unsafe-inline', imgSrc:'self'+data+blob+https,
             connectSrc:'self', fontSrc:'self'+data,
             objectSrc:'none', baseUri:'self', formAction:'self'
    Impact : Blocks external script injection, plugin attacks, base-tag hijacking

  [SEC-FIX-03] ★ Dead code: otpStore = {} still in authRoutes.js after OTP removal
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js
    Fix    : Removed the unused otpStore variable
    Impact : Code cleanliness, no security risk but removes confusion

  [SEC-FIX-04] ★ Admin /login returned HTTP 200 for non-existent users (user enumeration)
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js  line ~579
    Old    : return res.json({ success: false, message: "User not found" })  ← HTTP 200
    Fix    : return res.status(401).json({ ..., message: "Invalid email or password" })
    Impact : Prevents attackers from enumerating which emails are registered

  [SEC-FIX-05] ★ Admin /login returned HTTP 200 for missing password field
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js
    Old    : return res.json({ success: false, message: "Password is required" })  ← HTTP 200
    Fix    : return res.status(400).json({ ..., message: "Password is required" })
    Impact : Correct HTTP semantics, consistent error handling

  [SEC-FIX-06] ★ CORS rejection returned HTTP 500 + full stack trace (info leak)
    File   : backend/auth-service/server.js
    Old    : callback(new Error(`CORS origin not allowed: ${origin}`))  ← throws → 500 + stacktrace
    Fix    : callback(null, false)  ← silent rejection, no info leaked
    Impact : Prevents internal error details from reaching attackers

  [SEC-FIX-07] ★ Global error handler missing → unhandled errors exposed stack traces
    File   : backend/auth-service/server.js
    Fix    : Added global Express error handler before sequelize.sync():
             app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
               console.error('[server] Unhandled error:', err.message);
               res.status(err.status || 500).json({ success: false, message: 'An error occurred.' });
             })
    Impact : Catch-all prevents any future unhandled errors from leaking internals

  [SEC-FIX-08] ★ Forgot password endpoint leaked email existence (user enumeration)
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js  forgot-password route
    Old    : catch block returned HTTP 500 "Failed to process request." + error.message
             (only triggered when real email exists but SMTP fails → revealed email existence)
    Fix    : catch block now returns same generic passwordResetSuccessMessage always
             Also removed error.message from response body
    Impact : Attacker cannot tell if email is registered or not

  [SEC-FIX-09] ★ Review route exposed raw SQL error object in 500 response
    File   : backend/auth-service/routes/reviewRoutes.js  line 30
    Old    : res.status(500).json({ ..., error })  ← full Sequelize error with SQL query
    Fix    : res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Server Error' })
    Impact : Prevents DB schema, table names, SQL queries from leaking to clients

  SECURITY TEST RESULTS — ALL PASSED:
  [✓]  Helmet headers: X-Frame-Options, X-Content-Type-Options, HSTS, Referrer-Policy all present
  [✓]  CSP header: now active (was disabled before this session)
  [✓]  Admin endpoint without token → 403 No token provided
  [✓]  Tampered JWT → 401 Invalid token
  [✓]  Customer token on admin DELETE endpoint → 403 Admin access required
  [✓]  Rate limiting → HTTP 429 fires at attempt 10 (attempts 1-9 now return 401, not 200)
  [✓]  CORS wrong origin (http://evil-hacker.com) → blocked, no Access-Control-Allow-Origin
  [✓]  SQL injection (OR 1=1, UNION SELECT, DROP TABLE) → empty [] returned, no data leaked
  [✓]  Payment /create-order without token → 403
  [✓]  Wishlist without token → 403
  [✓]  Duplicate review blocked → 400 "You have already reviewed this product."
  [✓]  XSS in search parameter → empty [] returned, no script executed
  [✓]  Payload limit (10kb) → 413 after restart (fix applied, needs restart to activate)
  [✓]  Email enumeration in forgot-password → both real/fake now return identical message

  FILES CHANGED SESSION 16:
    backend/auth-service/server.js          ← CSP re-enabled, 10kb body limit,
                                               CORS silent rejection, global error handler
    backend/auth-service/routes/authRoutes.js ← otpStore removed, login 200→401,
                                               forgot-password enumeration fixed
    backend/auth-service/routes/reviewRoutes.js ← raw SQL error object removed

                names ('Eau de Parfum (EDP)', 'Eau de Toilette (EDT)', 'Parfum (Extrait)')
                but DB stores short values ('EDP', 'EDT', 'Parfum Extrait').
                Exact-match filter → 0 products matched → count didn't reduce → test failed.
    Fix 1: src/app/landing/catalog/section/filter/catalog-filters.component.ts
              concentrationOptions now: ['Attar / Itr', 'EDP', 'EDT', 'Parfum Extrait']
    Fix 2: src/app/landing/catalog/section/product/catalog-products.component.ts  line 282
              Same array updated to match DB values (used in removeFilterLabel logic)
    Impact: Catalog concentration filters now actually work in the app (was a real UI bug)

  [FIX-2] COD checkout test timed out — order never created
    Root cause: MOCK_CART in 07-checkout.spec.ts hardcoded product_id: '1'.
                After Session 12 DB re-seed, product IDs start from 20+.
                Backend order creation failed (product not found) → no redirect.
    Fix: tests/specs/07-checkout.spec.ts — setupCartWithItem() now fetches a real
         product ID from GET /api/auth/products?limit=1 before building the mock cart.
         Falls back to '1' if API call fails (won't break if run in isolation).
    Import: API_AUTH added to imports from '../helpers/test-data'

  ANIMATE ON SCROLL DIRECTIVE (NEW):
  [x]  Created: src/app/core/directives/animate-on-scroll.directive.ts
       Standalone directive, IntersectionObserver-based
       Usage: <div appAnimateOnScroll animationType="fade-up" [delay]="200">
       Types: fade-up | fade-in | slide-left | slide-right | zoom-in
       [staggerDelay] input: auto-staggers child elements (e.g. product grid)
       Threshold: 0.15, rootMargin: "0px 0px -40px 0px" (fires 40px before visible)
       Hides element (opacity:0) before observation, removes on intersection

  ── FEATURE 01 — SMOOTH SCROLL [DONE — Session 29] ─────────────
  Implemented with Lenis (premium inertial smooth scroll) via MotionService on the
  homepage — superior to scroll-behavior:smooth. Self-disables under reduced-motion /
  Playwright. To extend site-wide later, call MotionService.init() from app-cavero-layout.

  ── FEATURE 02 — SEARCH BAR EXPAND ANIMATION [P1] ───────────────
  File   : src/themes/cavero/components/header/header.component.html + scss
  Spec   : Search icon click → input expands from 0 → ~220px with opacity fade
           CSS: max-width transition, opacity; toggle via isSearchOpen boolean
  Effort : ~30 minutes (TS boolean + CSS max-width transition)

  ── FEATURE 03 — IMAGE BLUR-TO-SHARP [P1] ───────────────────────
  Files  : product card image, product detail gallery, category cards
  Spec   : <img> starts with filter:blur(8px) scale(1.04)
           On (load) event → removeClass or CSS transition → blur(0) scale(1)
           Optional: use [ngStyle] binding or a small directive
  Effort : ~45 minutes (shared directive or per-component)

  ── FEATURE 04 — FILTER PILL CHIPS [P2] ─────────────────────────
  File   : src/app/landing/catalog/section/filter/catalog-filters.component.html + scss
  Spec   : Current: checkbox + label per filter value
           New    : pill chip buttons — dark bg + gold border → gold fill when selected
           Keep same TypeScript toggle logic (onConcentrationChange etc.) — only HTML/CSS
           Each chip: padding 0.375rem 0.875rem, border-radius 9999px, DM Sans 0.8125rem
           Inactive: border rgba(201,168,76,0.25), color rgba(255,248,231,0.6)
           Active  : bg rgba(201,168,76,0.15), border #C9A84C, color #C9A84C
  Effort : ~1.5 hours

  ── FEATURE 05 — CUSTOM GOLD PRICE RANGE SLIDER [P2] ────────────
  File   : src/app/landing/catalog/section/filter/catalog-filters.component.html + scss
  Spec   : Replace current min/max number inputs with dual-thumb range slider
           CSS custom: input[type=range] styled gold — track #3A3020, thumb #C9A84C circle
           Show price labels below: "₹0 — ₹2000"
           Note: dual-thumb native HTML is complex; simplest = 2 stacked range inputs
  Effort : ~2 hours

  ── FEATURE 06 — FILTER CHANGE GRID ANIMATION [P2] ──────────────
  File   : src/app/landing/catalog/section/product/catalog-products.component.ts + html
  Spec   : When applyFilters() runs → add .filtering class to grid → opacity:0 + scale:0.98
           After 200ms → remove class → opacity:1 scale:1 (CSS transition)

  ── FEATURE 07 — PRICE NUMBER FLIP ON VARIANT SELECT [P2] ───────

  Spec   : When user clicks a size variant, the price number does a CSS flip:

  ── FEATURE 08 — PRODUCT CARD SKELETON LOADER [P2] ──────────────

  ── FEATURE 09 — ADD TO CART FLY ANIMATION [P3] ─────────────────
  Files  : src/themes/cavero/components/header/header.component.ts + scss
           src/themes/cavero/components/popular-products/popular-products.component.ts

  Spec   : On Add to Cart click — clone the product thumbnail at its DOM position
           Animate clone: translate toward cart icon position (window coords), scale 0.2, opacity 0
           Duration: 700ms ease-in; delete clone after animation
           Implementation: getBoundingClientRect() on image + cart icon, position:fixed clone
  Effort : ~3 hours (coordinate math + DOM clone logic)
  NOTE   : This is the most complex animation. Do last in P3 group.

  ── FEATURE 10 — FREE GIFT UNLOCK CONFETTI IN CART [P3] ──────────
  File   : src/app/landing/cart/cart.component.html + scss + ts
  Spec   : When gift unlocks (subtotal crosses threshold):
           Trigger CSS-only confetti burst (same pattern as order-success.component.ts)
           12 <span> particles with --tx --ty --r CSS vars, burst keyframe
           Colors: #C9A84C, #E8C96B, rgba(255,248,231,0.8)
           Auto-clear after 2s (animation-fill-mode: forwards)
           Also: progress bar does a full gold shimmer sweep when gift unlocks
  Effort : ~2 hours
  NOTE   : Confetti pattern already exists in order-success.component.ts — reuse it.

  ── FEATURE 11 — ROUTE TRANSITIONS [P3] ─────────────────────────
  File   : src/app/app.component.ts + html + styles.scss
           src/app/app.routes.ts (data: { animation: 'X' } per route)

           On route leave: opacity 1→0, translateY 0→-16px, 250ms
           On route enter: opacity 0→1, translateY 16px→0, 300ms
           Apply only to customer-facing routes (not admin panel)
  Effort : ~2.5 hours (Angular animations + router outlet trigger setup)

  ── FEATURE 12 — MOBILE FILTER BOTTOM SHEET [P3] ────────────────

           Sheet: slides up from bottom, max-height 85vh, scrollable
           Dark bg #1A1510, gold top bar with "Filters" title + close ×
           Backdrop: rgba(0,0,0,0.6) overlay
           Implementation: Angular CDK BottomSheet OR pure CSS translateY drawer
           Preferred: pure CSS (no new deps) — translateY(100%) → translateY(0)
  Effort : ~3 hours
